function fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const explicit=Number(p.suspension_remaining??p.suspension_games_remaining??p.ban_games_remaining??0),status=String(p.suspension_status??'').toLowerCase(),until=p.banned_until??p.suspension_until??null;if(explicit>0||/(suspend|ban|unavailable)/.test(status)||until){const bits=['Suspended'];if(explicit>0)bits.push(`${explicit} league match${explicit===1?'':'es'} remaining`);else if(until)bits.push(`until ${fmFmtStatusDate(until)}`);const reason=String(p.suspension_detail??'').trim();if(reason&&!/^active fm suspension$/i.test(reason))bits.push(reason);out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:bits.join(' · ')});continue}const hist=[...(p.history||[])].filter(h=>h.date).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));if(!hist.length)continue;const incidents=[];for(const h of hist){const rc=Number(h.red_cards??h.rc??0),yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);if(rc>0)incidents.push({date:String(h.date),ban:1,reason:yc>=2?'second-yellow red':'red card'});}let yellows=[];for(const h of hist){const yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);for(let i=0;i<yc;i++)yellows.push(String(h.date));}for(const [threshold,ban] of [[5,1],[10,2],[15,3]])if(yellows.length>=threshold)incidents.push({date:yellows[threshold-1],ban,reason:`${threshold} yellow cards`});incidents.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));for(const inc of incidents){const served=fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,p.club,inc.date),remaining=Math.max(0,Number(inc.ban)-served);if(remaining>0){out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:`Suspended · ${remaining} league match${remaining===1?'':'es'} remaining · ${inc.reason}`});break}}}return out}

  async ensure(){

  u64(b,p){return Number(new DataView(b.buffer,b.byteOffset,b.byteLength).getBigUint64(p,true))},
  rfind(h,n){outer:for(let i=h.length-n.length;i>=0;i--){for(let j=0;j<n.length;j++)if(h[i+j]!==n[j])continue outer;return i}return -1},

    if(!file||!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.fm'))throw new Error('Please select a Football Manager .fm save file.');
    const report=(text,pct)=>progress(text,Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(pct))));

    const timer=setInterval(()=>{estimated=Math.min(93,estimated+1);report('Building FM Fantasy database…',estimated)},1400);
    let json;
    try{json=await py.runPythonAsync('browser_build_payload_from_fs(browser_fingerprint, browser_rich_names_json, browser_preferred_league)')}
    finally{clearInterval(timer)}
    report('Finalising imported database…',96);const payload=JSON.parse(json);
    // Fast coverage guard: one import pass only. We do NOT recursively re-scan/rebuild the save.
    // But we also never call partial player-history recovery "complete" just because some rows were found.
    payload.meta=payload.meta||{};
    {const played=Number(payload.meta.played_results||0),rich=Number(payload.meta.rich_matches||0),missing=Math.max(0,played-rich),coverage=played?rich/played:1;
      payload.meta.rich_match_coverage=Math.round(coverage*1000)/1000;
      payload.meta.rich_matches_missing=missing;
      payload.meta.history_coverage_status=missing===0?'complete':(rich>0?'partial':'missing');
      payload.meta.history_recovery_policy='single-archive-scan + cached identity propagation';

    }
    report('Import parsed successfully.',98);return{duplicate:false,fingerprint:fp,payload}
  }

const FM_DEBUG_KEY='fmfantasy_last_debug_v1';
let FM_DEBUG={version:'v25-pricing-guardrails',startedAt:null,mode:null,file:null,events:[],lastError:null,lastMeta:null};
try{const saved=localStorage.getItem(FM_DEBUG_KEY);if(saved)FM_DEBUG=Object.assign(FM_DEBUG,JSON.parse(saved))}catch(_e){}
function fmDebugPersist(){try{localStorage.setItem(FM_DEBUG_KEY,JSON.stringify(FM_DEBUG))}catch(_e){}}
function fmDebugReset(file,mode){
  FM_DEBUG={version:'v25-pricing-guardrails',startedAt:new Date().toISOString(),mode,file:file?{name:file.name,size:file.size,lastModified:file.lastModified,type:file.type||''}:null,events:[],lastError:null,lastMeta:null};
  fmDebugAdd('info','Import started');
}
function fmDebugAdd(type,message,extra=null){
  const item={at:new Date().toISOString(),type:String(type||'info'),message:String(message??'')};
  if(extra!==null&&extra!==undefined)item.extra=extra;
  FM_DEBUG.events.push(item);if(FM_DEBUG.events.length>250)FM_DEBUG.events=FM_DEBUG.events.slice(-250);fmDebugPersist();
}
function fmDebugSafeError(e){return{name:String(e?.name||'Error'),message:String(e?.message||e||'Unknown error'),stack:String(e?.stack||'')}}

  parseManifest(raw){let p=0;const rs=()=>{const n=this.u32(raw,p);p+=4;const s=this.td.decode(raw.subarray(p,p+n));p+=n;return s};const save=rs(),n=this.u32(raw,p);p+=4,items=[];const one=(group=null)=>{let parts=[];while(true){const s=rs();parts.push(s);if(s.startsWith('.'))break}const offset=this.u64(raw,p),stored=this.u64(raw,p+8),plain=this.u64(raw,p+16);p+=40;return{name:parts.join(''),offset,stored,plain,group}};for(let i=0;i<n;i++)items.push(one());const subn=this.u32(raw,p);p+=4;for(let i=0;i<subn;i++){const g=rs(),cnt=this.u32(raw,p);p+=4;for(let j=0;j<cnt;j++)items.push(one(g))}return{save,items}},
  async fingerprint(buf){
    try{if(globalThis.crypto&&crypto.subtle){const h=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',buf);return[...new Uint8Array(h)].map(x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('')}}catch(e){}
    const a=new Uint8Array(buf);let h1=0x811c9dc5|0,h2=0x9e3779b9|0;for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++){h1=Math.imul(h1^a[i],0x01000193);h2=Math.imul((h2+a[i]+i)|0,0x85ebca6b)}return((h1>>>0).toString(16).padStart(8,'0')+(h2>>>0).toString(16).padStart(8,'0')+'-'+a.length)
  },
  containsAscii(buf,text){const n=this.te.encode(text);outer:for(let i=0;i<=buf.length-n.length;i++){for(let j=0;j<n.length;j++)if(buf[i+j]!==n[j])continue outer;return true}return false},
  richStatCount(buf,limit=40){let p=0,c=0;while(p<buf.length-214){p=buf.indexOf(2,p);if(p<0||p+214>buf.length)break;const rating=buf[p+136]|(buf[p+137]<<8);if(buf[p+5]>=1&&buf[p+5]<=99&&buf[p+7]===2&&buf[p+20]<=12&&buf[p+21]<=20&&buf[p+50]<=30&&buf[p+51]<=30&&buf[p+52]<=30&&buf[p+56]<=12&&buf[p+63]<=5&&buf[p+64]<=2&&buf[p+67]<=130&&buf[p+71]<=130&&rating>=400&&rating<=1000){c++;if(c>=limit)return c;p+=140}else p++}return c},
  async importFile(file,progress=()=>{},knownFingerprint=null,preferredLeague=null){
    if(!file||!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.fm'))throw new Error('Please select a Football Manager .fm save file.');
    const report=(text,pct)=>progress(text,Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(pct))));
    report('Reading FM save…',2);let rawBuffer=await file.arrayBuffer();report('Reading FM save…',7);
    let b=new Uint8Array(rawBuffer);const fp=await this.fingerprint(rawBuffer);report('Checking save fingerprint…',11);let rememberedLeague=null;try{rememberedLeague=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fmfantasy_league_by_fingerprint_v1')||'{}')[fp]||null}catch(_e){};preferredLeague=preferredLeague||rememberedLeague;
    if(knownFingerprint&&knownFingerprint===fp)return{duplicate:true,fingerprint:fp};
    const marker=new Uint8Array([2,1,102,109,102,46]),magicBytes=new Uint8Array([0x28,0xb5,0x2f,0xfd]);const tail=b.subarray(Math.max(0,b.length-4000000));const rel=this.rfind(tail,marker),magic=this.find(tail,magicBytes,Math.max(0,rel+marker.length));if(rel<0||magic<0)throw new Error('This does not look like a supported FM26 save (manifest not found).');
    report('Reading FM archive…',15);await this.ensure();report('Reading FM archive…',22);
    const manifest=window.fzstd.decompress(tail.subarray(magic));const {items}=this.parseManifest(manifest);const by=new Map(items.map(x=>[x.name,x]));const fixM=by.get('fix_man.dat'),dbM=by.get('game_db.dat'),injM=by.get('injury_manager.dat'),discM=by.get('discipline.dat');if(!fixM||!dbM)throw new Error('Required FM database/fixture members were not found in this save.');
    const get=m=>{const src=b.subarray(26+m.offset,26+m.offset+m.stored);return window.fzstd.decompress(src,new Uint8Array(m.plain))};const py=this.pyodide;
    report('Extracting league fixture calendar…',29);let fix=get(fixM);py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/fix_man.dat',fix);fix=null;report('Fixture calendar extracted.',34);
    const candidates=items.filter(m=>{if(!(m.plain>0&&m.plain<=64000000))return false;const n=m.name.toLowerCase();return m.name==='news.dat'||m.name==='play_fixture_manager.dat'||n.endsWith('.scm')||n.endsWith('.apm')||n.endsWith('.pkm')||n.includes('match')||n.includes('fixture')||n.includes('history')||n.includes('form')||n.includes('stat')});const richNames=[],richProbe=[];
    for(let i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){const m=candidates[i];let out=get(m);const labelHit=this.containsAscii(out,'Sky Bet Championship')||this.containsAscii(out,'EFL Championship')||this.containsAscii(out,'Premier League')||this.containsAscii(out,'English Premier Division');const statCount=this.richStatCount(out,40);if(labelHit||statCount>=40){const idx=richNames.length;richNames.push(m.name);richProbe.push({name:m.name,plain:m.plain,labelHit,statSignature:statCount>=40,statCount});py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/rich_'+idx+'.bin',out)}out=null;const pct=35+(candidates.length?25*(i+1)/candidates.length:25);if(i%4===0||i===candidates.length-1)report('Scanning retained match detail… '+(i+1)+'/'+candidates.length,pct)}
    report('Extracting players, clubs and results…',63);let db=get(dbM);py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/game_db.dat',db);db=null;if(injM){let ib=get(injM);py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/injury_manager.dat',ib);ib=null}if(discM){let sb=get(discM);py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/discipline.dat',sb);sb=null}b=null;rawBuffer=null;report('Players, clubs, results and availability extracted.',69);
    py.globals.set('browser_fingerprint',fp);py.globals.set('browser_rich_names_json',JSON.stringify(richNames));py.globals.set('browser_rich_probe_json',JSON.stringify(richProbe));py.globals.set('browser_preferred_league',preferredLeague||'');
    let estimated=70;report('Building FM Fantasy database…',estimated);
    const timer=setInterval(()=>{estimated=Math.min(93,estimated+1);report('Building FM Fantasy database…',estimated)},1400);
    let json;
    try{json=await py.runPythonAsync('browser_build_payload_from_fs(browser_fingerprint, browser_rich_names_json, browser_preferred_league)')}
    finally{clearInterval(timer)}
    report('Finalising imported database…',96);const payload=JSON.parse(json);
    // Fast coverage guard: one import pass only. We do NOT recursively re-scan/rebuild the save.
    // But we also never call partial player-history recovery "complete" just because some rows were found.
    payload.meta=payload.meta||{};
    {const played=Number(payload.meta.played_results||0),rich=Number(payload.meta.rich_matches||0),missing=Math.max(0,played-rich),coverage=played?rich/played:1;
      payload.meta.rich_match_coverage=Math.round(coverage*1000)/1000;
      payload.meta.rich_matches_missing=missing;
      payload.meta.history_coverage_status=missing===0?'complete':(rich>0?'partial':'missing');
      payload.meta.history_recovery_policy='single-archive-scan + cached identity propagation';
      if(missing>0&&typeof fmDebugAdd==='function')fmDebugAdd('warning',`Historical player coverage incomplete: ${rich}/${played} played league matches decoded (${missing} missing). Import accepted as partial; archive rescans are disabled. Cached identity propagation already ran during this import.`);
    }
    report('Import parsed successfully.',98);return{duplicate:false,fingerprint:fp,payload}
  }
};

const FM_DEBUG_KEY='fmfantasy_last_debug_v1';
let FM_DEBUG={version:'v25-pricing-guardrails',startedAt:null,mode:null,file:null,events:[],lastError:null,lastMeta:null};
try{const saved=localStorage.getItem(FM_DEBUG_KEY);if(saved)FM_DEBUG=Object.assign(FM_DEBUG,JSON.parse(saved))}catch(_e){}
function fmDebugPersist(){try{localStorage.setItem(FM_DEBUG_KEY,JSON.stringify(FM_DEBUG))}catch(_e){}}
function fmDebugReset(file,mode){
  FM_DEBUG={version:'v25-pricing-guardrails',startedAt:new Date().toISOString(),mode,file:file?{name:file.name,size:file.size,lastModified:file.lastModified,type:file.type||''}:null,events:[],lastError:null,lastMeta:null};
  fmDebugAdd('info','Import started');
}
function fmDebugAdd(type,message,extra=null){
  const item={at:new Date().toISOString(),type:String(type||'info'),message:String(message??'')};
  if(extra!==null&&extra!==undefined)item.extra=extra;
  FM_DEBUG.events.push(item);if(FM_DEBUG.events.length>250)FM_DEBUG.events=FM_DEBUG.events.slice(-250);fmDebugPersist();
}
function fmDebugSafeError(e){return{name:String(e?.name||'Error'),message:String(e?.message||e||'Unknown error'),stack:String(e?.stack||'')}}
function fmDebugText(){
  const d=FM_DEBUG||{};
  const lines=[
    'FM FANTASY IMPORT DEBUG',
    '=======================',
    `Generated: ${new Date().toISOString()}`,
    `Build: ${d.version||'unknown'}`,
    `Import mode: ${d.mode||'none'}`,
    `Started: ${d.startedAt||'not recorded'}`,
    `File: ${d.file?.name||'not recorded'}`,
    `File size: ${d.file?.size??'not recorded'} bytes`,
    `File last modified: ${d.file?.lastModified?new Date(d.file.lastModified).toISOString():'not recorded'}`,
    `Page protocol: ${location.protocol}`,
    `Online: ${navigator.onLine}`,
    `Secure context: ${globalThis.isSecureContext}`,
    `User agent: ${navigator.userAgent}`,
    `fzstd loaded: ${!!window.fzstd}`,
    `Pyodide loader loaded: ${!!window.loadPyodide}`,
    `Pyodide runtime ready: ${!!FM_RUNTIME.pyodide}`,
    '',
    'EVENTS',
    '------'
  ];
  for(const x of (d.events||[])){lines.push(`[${x.at}] ${String(x.type||'info').toUpperCase()}: ${x.message}`);if(x.extra!==undefined)lines.push(typeof x.extra==='string'?x.extra:JSON.stringify(x.extra,null,2))}
  if(d.lastMeta){lines.push('','LAST PAYLOAD META','-----------------',JSON.stringify(d.lastMeta,null,2))}
  if(d.lastError){lines.push('','LAST ERROR','----------',`${d.lastError.name}: ${d.lastError.message}`,d.lastError.stack||'(no stack)')}
  lines.push('','Database policy: Gameweek Update Import is monotonic — previously imported played results, rich match detail and player rows are retained; updates add/refresh data and do not delete history.',
    '',
    'Privacy note: this report contains diagnostics and file metadata only; it does not contain the .fm save contents.');
  return lines.join('\n');
}
async function exportFMDebug(){
  const text=fmDebugText();
  try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);syncMessage('Debug copied to clipboard — paste it into ChatGPT.','ok');return}
  catch(copyErr){
    try{const blob=new Blob([text],{type:'text/plain'}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='FM_Fantasy_Debug.txt';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);syncMessage('Clipboard was blocked, so the debug report was downloaded instead.','ok')}
    catch(dlErr){syncMessage('Could not export debug: '+String(dlErr?.message||dlErr),'bad')}
  }
}

function fmDebugAdd(type,message,extra=null){

function fmDebugText(){
  const d=FM_DEBUG||{};
  const lines=[
    'FM FANTASY IMPORT DEBUG',
    '=======================',
    `Generated: ${new Date().toISOString()}`,
    `Build: ${d.version||'unknown'}`,
    `Import mode: ${d.mode||'none'}`,
    `Started: ${d.startedAt||'not recorded'}`,
    `File: ${d.file?.name||'not recorded'}`,
    `File size: ${d.file?.size??'not recorded'} bytes`,
    `File last modified: ${d.file?.lastModified?new Date(d.file.lastModified).toISOString():'not recorded'}`,
    `Page protocol: ${location.protocol}`,
    `Online: ${navigator.onLine}`,
    `Secure context: ${globalThis.isSecureContext}`,
    `User agent: ${navigator.userAgent}`,
    `fzstd loaded: ${!!window.fzstd}`,
    `Pyodide loader loaded: ${!!window.loadPyodide}`,
    `Pyodide runtime ready: ${!!FM_RUNTIME.pyodide}`,
    '',
    'EVENTS',
    '------'
  ];
  for(const x of (d.events||[])){lines.push(`[${x.at}] ${String(x.type||'info').toUpperCase()}: ${x.message}`);if(x.extra!==undefined)lines.push(typeof x.extra==='string'?x.extra:JSON.stringify(x.extra,null,2))}
  if(d.lastMeta){lines.push('','LAST PAYLOAD META','-----------------',JSON.stringify(d.lastMeta,null,2))}
  if(d.lastError){lines.push('','LAST ERROR','----------',`${d.lastError.name}: ${d.lastError.message}`,d.lastError.stack||'(no stack)')}
  lines.push('','Database policy: Gameweek Update Import is monotonic — previously imported played results, rich match detail and player rows are retained; updates add/refresh data and do not delete history.',
    '',
    'Privacy note: this report contains diagnostics and file metadata only; it does not contain the .fm save contents.');
  return lines.join('\n');
}
async function exportFMDebug(){
  const text=fmDebugText();
  try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);syncMessage('Debug copied to clipboard — paste it into ChatGPT.','ok');return}
  catch(copyErr){
    try{const blob=new Blob([text],{type:'text/plain'}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='FM_Fantasy_Debug.txt';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);syncMessage('Clipboard was blocked, so the debug report was downloaded instead.','ok')}
    catch(dlErr){syncMessage('Could not export debug: '+String(dlErr?.message||dlErr),'bad')}
  }
}
function fmFriendlyError(e){
  const raw=String(e?.message||e||'Import failed').trim();
  const lines=raw.split(/\r?\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const useful=[...lines].reverse().find(x=>!/^\^+$/.test(x)&&!x.startsWith('File ')&&!x.startsWith('Traceback'))||raw;
  return useful.length>220?useful.slice(0,217)+'…':useful;
}

    const a=new Uint8Array(buf);let h1=0x811c9dc5|0,h2=0x9e3779b9|0;for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++){h1=Math.imul(h1^a[i],0x01000193);h2=Math.imul((h2+a[i]+i)|0,0x85ebca6b)}return((h1>>>0).toString(16).padStart(8,'0')+(h2>>>0).toString(16).padStart(8,'0')+'-'+a.length)
  },
  containsAscii(buf,text){const n=this.te.encode(text);outer:for(let i=0;i<=buf.length-n.length;i++){for(let j=0;j<n.length;j++)if(buf[i+j]!==n[j])continue outer;return true}return false},
  richStatCount(buf,limit=40){let p=0,c=0;while(p<buf.length-214){p=buf.indexOf(2,p);if(p<0||p+214>buf.length)break;const rating=buf[p+136]|(buf[p+137]<<8);if(buf[p+5]>=1&&buf[p+5]<=99&&buf[p+7]===2&&buf[p+20]<=12&&buf[p+21]<=20&&buf[p+50]<=30&&buf[p+51]<=30&&buf[p+52]<=30&&buf[p+56]<=12&&buf[p+63]<=5&&buf[p+64]<=2&&buf[p+67]<=130&&buf[p+71]<=130&&rating>=400&&rating<=1000){c++;if(c>=limit)return c;p+=140}else p++}return c},
  async importFile(file,progress=()=>{},knownFingerprint=null,preferredLeague=null){
    if(!file||!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.fm'))throw new Error('Please select a Football Manager .fm save file.');
    const report=(text,pct)=>progress(text,Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(pct))));
    report('Reading FM save…',2);let rawBuffer=await file.arrayBuffer();report('Reading FM save…',7);
    let b=new Uint8Array(rawBuffer);const fp=await this.fingerprint(rawBuffer);report('Checking save fingerprint…',11);let rememberedLeague=null;try{rememberedLeague=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fmfantasy_league_by_fingerprint_v1')||'{}')[fp]||null}catch(_e){};preferredLeague=preferredLeague||rememberedLeague;
    if(knownFingerprint&&knownFingerprint===fp)return{duplicate:true,fingerprint:fp};
    const marker=new Uint8Array([2,1,102,109,102,46]),magicBytes=new Uint8Array([0x28,0xb5,0x2f,0xfd]);const tail=b.subarray(Math.max(0,b.length-4000000));const rel=this.rfind(tail,marker),magic=this.find(tail,magicBytes,Math.max(0,rel+marker.length));if(rel<0||magic<0)throw new Error('This does not look like a supported FM26 save (manifest not found).');
    report('Reading FM archive…',15);await this.ensure();report('Reading FM archive…',22);
    const manifest=window.fzstd.decompress(tail.subarray(magic));const {items}=this.parseManifest(manifest);const by=new Map(items.map(x=>[x.name,x]));const fixM=by.get('fix_man.dat'),dbM=by.get('game_db.dat'),injM=by.get('injury_manager.dat'),discM=by.get('discipline.dat');if(!fixM||!dbM)throw new Error('Required FM database/fixture members were not found in this save.');
    const get=m=>{const src=b.subarray(26+m.offset,26+m.offset+m.stored);return window.fzstd.decompress(src,new Uint8Array(m.plain))};const py=this.pyodide;
    report('Extracting league fixture calendar…',29);let fix=get(fixM);py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/fix_man.dat',fix);fix=null;report('Fixture calendar extracted.',34);
    const candidates=items.filter(m=>{if(!(m.plain>0&&m.plain<=64000000))return false;const n=m.name.toLowerCase();return m.name==='news.dat'||m.name==='play_fixture_manager.dat'||n.endsWith('.scm')||n.endsWith('.apm')||n.endsWith('.pkm')||n.includes('match')||n.includes('fixture')||n.includes('history')||n.includes('form')||n.includes('stat')});const richNames=[],richProbe=[];
    for(let i=0;i<candidates.length;i++){const m=candidates[i];let out=get(m);const labelHit=this.containsAscii(out,'Sky Bet Championship')||this.containsAscii(out,'EFL Championship')||this.containsAscii(out,'Premier League')||this.containsAscii(out,'English Premier Division');const statCount=this.richStatCount(out,40);if(labelHit||statCount>=40){const idx=richNames.length;richNames.push(m.name);richProbe.push({name:m.name,plain:m.plain,labelHit,statSignature:statCount>=40,statCount});py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/rich_'+idx+'.bin',out)}out=null;const pct=35+(candidates.length?25*(i+1)/candidates.length:25);if(i%4===0||i===candidates.length-1)report('Scanning retained match detail… '+(i+1)+'/'+candidates.length,pct)}
    report('Extracting players, clubs and results…',63);let db=get(dbM);py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/game_db.dat',db);db=null;if(injM){let ib=get(injM);py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/injury_manager.dat',ib);ib=null}if(discM){let sb=get(discM);py.FS.writeFile('/tmp/discipline.dat',sb);sb=null}b=null;rawBuffer=null;report('Players, clubs, results and availability extracted.',69);
    py.globals.set('browser_fingerprint',fp);py.globals.set('browser_rich_names_json',JSON.stringify(richNames));py.globals.set('browser_rich_probe_json',JSON.stringify(richProbe));py.globals.set('browser_preferred_league',preferredLeague||'');
    let estimated=70;report('Building FM Fantasy database…',estimated);
    const timer=setInterval(()=>{estimated=Math.min(93,estimated+1);report('Building FM Fantasy database…',estimated)},1400);
    let json;
    try{json=await py.runPythonAsync('browser_build_payload_from_fs(browser_fingerprint, browser_rich_names_json, browser_preferred_league)')}
    finally{clearInterval(timer)}
    report('Finalising imported database…',96);const payload=JSON.parse(json);
    // Fast coverage guard: one import pass only. We do NOT recursively re-scan/rebuild the save.
    // But we also never call partial player-history recovery "complete" just because some rows were found.
    payload.meta=payload.meta||{};
    {const played=Number(payload.meta.played_results||0),rich=Number(payload.meta.rich_matches||0),missing=Math.max(0,played-rich),coverage=played?rich/played:1;
      payload.meta.rich_match_coverage=Math.round(coverage*1000)/1000;
      payload.meta.rich_matches_missing=missing;
      payload.meta.history_coverage_status=missing===0?'complete':(rich>0?'partial':'missing');
      payload.meta.history_recovery_policy='single-archive-scan + cached identity propagation';
      if(missing>0&&typeof fmDebugAdd==='function')fmDebugAdd('warning',`Historical player coverage incomplete: ${rich}/${played} played league matches decoded (${missing} missing). Import accepted as partial; archive rescans are disabled. Cached identity propagation already ran during this import.`);
    }
    report('Import parsed successfully.',98);return{duplicate:false,fingerprint:fp,payload}
  }
};

const FM_DEBUG_KEY='fmfantasy_last_debug_v1';
let FM_DEBUG={version:'v25-pricing-guardrails',startedAt:null,mode:null,file:null,events:[],lastError:null,lastMeta:null};
try{const saved=localStorage.getItem(FM_DEBUG_KEY);if(saved)FM_DEBUG=Object.assign(FM_DEBUG,JSON.parse(saved))}catch(_e){}
function fmDebugPersist(){try{localStorage.setItem(FM_DEBUG_KEY,JSON.stringify(FM_DEBUG))}catch(_e){}}
function fmDebugReset(file,mode){
  FM_DEBUG={version:'v25-pricing-guardrails',startedAt:new Date().toISOString(),mode,file:file?{name:file.name,size:file.size,lastModified:file.lastModified,type:file.type||''}:null,events:[],lastError:null,lastMeta:null};
  fmDebugAdd('info','Import started');
}
function fmDebugAdd(type,message,extra=null){
  const item={at:new Date().toISOString(),type:String(type||'info'),message:String(message??'')};
  if(extra!==null&&extra!==undefined)item.extra=extra;
  FM_DEBUG.events.push(item);if(FM_DEBUG.events.length>250)FM_DEBUG.events=FM_DEBUG.events.slice(-250);fmDebugPersist();
}
function fmDebugSafeError(e){return{name:String(e?.name||'Error'),message:String(e?.message||e||'Unknown error'),stack:String(e?.stack||'')}}
function fmDebugText(){
  const d=FM_DEBUG||{};
  const lines=[
    'FM FANTASY IMPORT DEBUG',
    '=======================',
    `Generated: ${new Date().toISOString()}`,
    `Build: ${d.version||'unknown'}`,
    `Import mode: ${d.mode||'none'}`,
    `Started: ${d.startedAt||'not recorded'}`,
    `File: ${d.file?.name||'not recorded'}`,
    `File size: ${d.file?.size??'not recorded'} bytes`,
    `File last modified: ${d.file?.lastModified?new Date(d.file.lastModified).toISOString():'not recorded'}`,
    `Page protocol: ${location.protocol}`,
    `Online: ${navigator.onLine}`,
    `Secure context: ${globalThis.isSecureContext}`,
    `User agent: ${navigator.userAgent}`,
    `fzstd loaded: ${!!window.fzstd}`,
    `Pyodide loader loaded: ${!!window.loadPyodide}`,
    `Pyodide runtime ready: ${!!FM_RUNTIME.pyodide}`,
    '',
    'EVENTS',
    '------'
  ];
  for(const x of (d.events||[])){lines.push(`[${x.at}] ${String(x.type||'info').toUpperCase()}: ${x.message}`);if(x.extra!==undefined)lines.push(typeof x.extra==='string'?x.extra:JSON.stringify(x.extra,null,2))}
  if(d.lastMeta){lines.push('','LAST PAYLOAD META','-----------------',JSON.stringify(d.lastMeta,null,2))}
  if(d.lastError){lines.push('','LAST ERROR','----------',`${d.lastError.name}: ${d.lastError.message}`,d.lastError.stack||'(no stack)')}
  lines.push('','Database policy: Gameweek Update Import is monotonic — previously imported played results, rich match detail and player rows are retained; updates add/refresh data and do not delete history.',
    '',
    'Privacy note: this report contains diagnostics and file metadata only; it does not contain the .fm save contents.');
  return lines.join('\n');
}
async function exportFMDebug(){
  const text=fmDebugText();
  try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);syncMessage('Debug copied to clipboard — paste it into ChatGPT.','ok');return}
  catch(copyErr){
    try{const blob=new Blob([text],{type:'text/plain'}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='FM_Fantasy_Debug.txt';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);syncMessage('Clipboard was blocked, so the debug report was downloaded instead.','ok')}
    catch(dlErr){syncMessage('Could not export debug: '+String(dlErr?.message||dlErr),'bad')}
  }
}
function fmFriendlyError(e){
  const raw=String(e?.message||e||'Import failed').trim();
  const lines=raw.split(/\r?\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const useful=[...lines].reverse().find(x=>!/^\^+$/.test(x)&&!x.startsWith('File ')&&!x.startsWith('Traceback'))||raw;
  return useful.length>220?useful.slice(0,217)+'…':useful;
}

  async ensure(){

function fmDebugReset(file,mode){
  FM_DEBUG={version:'v25-pricing-guardrails',startedAt:new Date().toISOString(),mode,file:file?{name:file.name,size:file.size,lastModified:file.lastModified,type:file.type||''}:null,events:[],lastError:null,lastMeta:null};
  fmDebugAdd('info','Import started');
}
function fmDebugAdd(type,message,extra=null){
  const item={at:new Date().toISOString(),type:String(type||'info'),message:String(message??'')};
  if(extra!==null&&extra!==undefined)item.extra=extra;
  FM_DEBUG.events.push(item);if(FM_DEBUG.events.length>250)FM_DEBUG.events=FM_DEBUG.events.slice(-250);fmDebugPersist();
}

function fmFixtureKey(f){return `${f.home_tid}:${f.away_tid}`}
function fmDiff(oldP,newP){const oldf=new Map((oldP.fixtures||[]).map(f=>[fmFixtureKey(f),f])),newf=new Map((newP.fixtures||[]).map(f=>[fmFixtureKey(f),f]));const new_results=[],fixtures_rescheduled=[];for(const [k,f] of newf){const o=oldf.get(k);if(f.status==='played'&&(!o||o.status!=='played'))new_results.push({gameweek:f.gameweek,home:f.home,away:f.away,score:`${f.home_score}-${f.away_score}`});if(o&&(o.date!==f.date||o.gameweek!==f.gameweek))fixtures_rescheduled.push({home:f.home,away:f.away,from:o.date,to:f.date,gameweek:f.gameweek})}const oldp=new Map((oldP.players||[]).filter(p=>p.available!==false).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),newp=new Map((newP.players||[]).filter(p=>p.available!==false).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p]));const players_added=[],players_removed=[],players_moved=[];for(const [id,p] of newp){if(!oldp.has(id))players_added.push({pid:id,name:p.name,club:p.club});else if(oldp.get(id).club!==p.club)players_moved.push({pid:id,name:p.name,from:oldp.get(id).club,to:p.club})}for(const [id,p] of oldp)if(!newp.has(id))players_removed.push({pid:id,name:p.name,club:p.club});const oldRich=new Set((oldP.matches||[]).map(m=>`${m.home}|${m.away}`));const new_rich_matches=(newP.matches||[]).filter(m=>!oldRich.has(`${m.home}|${m.away}`)).length;return{new_results,new_rich_matches,players_added,players_removed,players_moved,fixtures_rescheduled}}
function fmBuildStars(players,latest){const out={};for(let gw=1;gw<=Math.max(1,latest||0);gw++){let best=null;const by={};for(const pos of ['GK','DEF','MID','FWD'])by[pos]=players.filter(p=>p.pos===pos).sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.weekly_points?.[String(gw)]||0)-Number(a.weekly_points?.[String(gw)]||0))||(b.fantasy_points-a.fantasy_points));for(let d=3;d<=5;d++)for(let m=2;m<=5;m++){const f=10-d-m;if(f<1||f>3)continue;const picks=[...by.GK.slice(0,1),...by.DEF.slice(0,d),...by.MID.slice(0,m),...by.FWD.slice(0,f)];if(picks.length!==11||new Set(picks.map(p=>p.id)).size!==11)continue;const pts=picks.reduce((s,p)=>s+Number(p.weekly_points?.[String(gw)]||0),0);if(!best||pts>best.points)best={points:pts,ids:picks.map(p=>p.id),formation:{GK:1,DEF:d,MID:m,FWD:f}}}if(best)out[String(gw)]=best}return out}

function fmDeepClone(x){try{return structuredClone(x)}catch(_e){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x))}}
function fmFixtureStableKey(f){if(f?.fixture_id!=null)return `id:${f.fixture_id}`;if(f?.home_tid!=null&&f?.away_tid!=null)return `teams:${f.home_tid}:${f.away_tid}`;return `names:${f?.home||''}|${f?.away||''}`}
function fmRichMatchKey(m){if(m?.fixture_id!=null)return `id:${m.fixture_id}`;return `${m?.home||''}|${m?.away||''}|${m?.date||''}`}
function fmMergeKnownObject(oldObj,newObj){const out=fmDeepClone(oldObj||{});for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(newObj||{}))if(v!==undefined&&v!==null)out[k]=fmDeepClone(v);return out}
function fmMergePlayerRows(oldRows,newRows){const out=new Map();for(const r of (oldRows||[]))out.set(String(r.player_id??r.pid??r.id),fmDeepClone(r));for(const r of (newRows||[])){const k=String(r.player_id??r.pid??r.id),prev=out.get(k);out.set(k,prev?fmMergeKnownObject(prev,r):fmDeepClone(r))}return [...out.values()]}
function fmMergeHistoricalFixtures(oldP,payload){

}

   fresh.set(k,om);retained++;

function newsRow(item,type){if(type==='price')return `<div class="newsRow" ${item.pid?`data-open-player="${esc(item.pid)}"`:'' } data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.club} ${item.pos}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.club)}</span><span>${esc(item.pos)}</span><span><b>£${Number(item.old_price).toFixed(1)}</b> → <b class="${item.new_price>item.old_price?'newsPriceUp':'newsPriceDown'}">£${Number(item.new_price).toFixed(1)}</b></span></div>`;if(type==='transfer')return `<div class="newsRow transfer" data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.old_club||''} ${item.new_club||item.club||''} ${item.pos||''}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.old_club||item.from||'Outside league')}</span><span>${esc(item.new_club||item.to||item.club||'')}</span><span>${esc(item.value||'—')}</span><span>${esc(item.pos||'')}</span><span>£${Number(item.price||0).toFixed(1)}m</span></div>`;return `<div class="newsRow status" ${item.pid?`data-open-player="${esc(item.pid)}"`:'' } data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.club} ${item.pos} ${item.detail||''}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.club)}</span><span>${esc(item.pos)}</span><span>${esc(item.detail||'Update')}</span></div>`}
function renderNewsCard(id,title,items,type){const el=$(id);if(!el)return;items=items||[];const icons={newsTransfers:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 8h13m0 0-3-3m3 3-3 3M19 16H6m0 0 3 3m-3-3 3-3"/></svg>',newsPriceUp:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 17 6-6 4 4 6-7m-5 0h5v5"/></svg>',newsPriceDown:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 7 6 6 4-4 6 7m-5 0h5v-5"/></svg>',newsInjuries:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="5" y="6" width="14" height="14" rx="2"/><path d="M9 6V4h6v2m-3 4v6m-3-3h6"/></svg>',newsSuspensions:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="m6 6 12 12"/></svg>'},preview=items.slice(0,5),rest=items.slice(5),key=id+'Full',head=type==='transfer'&&items.length?'<div class="newsTransferHead"><span>Player</span><span>Old club</span><span>New club</span><span>FM value</span><span>Pos</span><span>FPL price</span></div>':'',activeLabel=type==='status'?'Active now':'Latest import',emptyText=type==='status'?'No active players.':'No changes this import.',search=items.length?`<input class="ctrl newsSearch" data-news-search="${id}" placeholder="Search player or club…">`:'',rows=preview.length?preview.map(x=>newsRow(x,type)).join(''):`<div class="newsEmpty"><span class="newsEmptyIcon">${type==='status'?'＋':'•'}</span><span>${emptyText}</span></div>`,more=rest.length?`<div id="${key}" class="newsHidden">${rest.map(x=>newsRow(x,type)).join('')}</div><button class="btn dark newsMore" data-news-toggle="${key}" data-news-count="${items.length}">View all ${items.length}</button>`:'';el.innerHTML=`<div class="newsHead"><div class="newsTitleWrap"><span class="newsIcon">${icons[id]||icons.newsTransfers}</span><div><h3 style="margin:0">${esc(title)}</h3><div class="muted">${activeLabel}${type==='transfer'?' · senior/first-team only':''}</div></div></div><div class="newsCount">${items.length}</div></div><div class="newsRows">${search}${head}${rows}${more}</div>`}
function filterNewsCard(id,q){const el=$(id);if(!el)return;const term=String(q||'').trim().toLowerCase(),rows=[...el.querySelectorAll('[data-news-text]')],full=$(id+'Full'),toggle=el.querySelector('[data-news-toggle]');if(term){if(full)full.classList.remove('newsHidden');rows.forEach(r=>r.style.display=(r.dataset.newsText||'').includes(term)?'':'none');if(toggle)toggle.style.display='none'}else{rows.forEach(r=>r.style.display='');if(full)full.classList.add('newsHidden');if(toggle){toggle.style.display='';toggle.textContent=`View all ${toggle.dataset.newsCount}`}}}
function renderNews(){const _newsCleared=(()=>{try{return localStorage.getItem('fmFantasyCloudDatabaseCleared')==='1'||sessionStorage.getItem('fmFantasyCloudDatabaseCleared')==='1'}catch(_){return false}})();const n=_newsCleared?null:state.news;if($('newsStamp'))$('newsStamp').textContent=n?(n.initial?`Database imported · active injuries/suspensions shown · transfer and price-change news begins after the first Gameweek update.`:`Latest Gameweek import · GW ${n.gameweek||'—'} · ${new Date(n.generatedAt).toLocaleString('en-GB')}`):'Season import establishes the database. Active injuries/suspensions will appear immediately; other news starts after the first Gameweek update.';renderNewsCard('newsTransfers','Transfers',n?.transfers,'transfer');renderNewsCard('newsPriceUp','Price increases',n?.priceIncreases,'price');renderNewsCard('newsPriceDown','Price decreases',n?.priceDecreases,'price');renderNewsCard('newsInjuries','Injuries',_newsCleared?[]:(n?.injuries||state.activeStatuses?.injuries),'status');renderNewsCard('newsSuspensions','Suspensions',_newsCleared?[]:(n?.suspensions||state.activeStatuses?.suspensions),'status')}

function applyImportedPayload(payload,mode='load'){try{if(payload&&Array.isArray(payload.players)&&payload.players.length){localStorage.removeItem('fmFantasyCloudDatabaseCleared');sessionStorage.removeItem('fmFantasyCloudDatabaseCleared')}}catch(_){}
 const matchByFixture=new Map((payload.matches||[]).map(m=>[m.fixture_id,m.id||m.match_id]));
 PLAYERS.splice(0,PLAYERS.length,...(payload.players||[]));
 MATCHES.splice(0,MATCHES.length,...(payload.matches||[]).map(m=>Object.assign({},m,{id:m.id||m.match_id})));
 SEASON_FIXTURES.splice(0,SEASON_FIXTURES.length,...(payload.fixtures||[]).map(f=>Object.assign({},f,{sid:f.fixture_id,match_id:matchByFixture.get(f.fixture_id)||null,time:f.status==='future'?'Scheduled':'FT'})));
 CHAMP_TABLE.splice(0,CHAMP_TABLE.length,...(payload.table||[]));
 for(const k of Object.keys(STAR_TEAMS))delete STAR_TEAMS[k];Object.assign(STAR_TEAMS,payload.star_teams||{});
 for(const k of Object.keys(META))delete META[k];Object.assign(META,{named_players:0,hidden_unresolved:0,completed_gameweek:0,current_gameweek:1,total_matches_extracted:0},payload.meta||{});META.named_players=PLAYERS.filter(p=>p.visible&&p.available!==false).length;META.total_matches_extracted=MATCHES.length;
 if(mode==='load'&&stored&&stored.bank===undefined){const held=(state.squad||[]).map(pby).filter(Boolean).reduce((a,p)=>a+Number(p.price||0),0);state.bank=Math.round(Math.max(0,100-held)*10)/10;state.lockedBank=state.bank}
 if(mode==='season'){
   const leagues=state.leagues||[];
   state=Object.assign({},DEFAULT,{leagues,completedGameweek:META.completed_gameweek,currentGameweek:META.current_gameweek,bank:100,lockedBank:100,news:null});
   state.chips=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT.chips));state.news=fmBuildInitialNews(payload);
 }else if(!state.teamConfirmed){state.completedGameweek=META.completed_gameweek;state.currentGameweek=META.current_gameweek}
 else syncManagerProgressFromHistory();
 if(mode==='load'&&!state.news){state.news=fmBuildInitialNews(payload)}
 fixtureViewGW=(state.teamConfirmed?state.currentGameweek:META.current_gameweek)||1;starViewGW=Math.max(1,META.completed_gameweek||1);teamViewGW=state.teamConfirmed?state.currentGameweek:(META.current_gameweek||1);
 fmRecalculateBank();refreshCompetitionUI();refreshClubFilters();save();renderAll();
}
function syncMessage(text,kind=''){const el=$('syncStatus');el.textContent=text;el.className='syncStatus '+kind}
function syncProgress(pct,label='',bad=false){const n=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(Number(pct)||0))),wrap=$('syncProgress');wrap.classList.add('show');wrap.classList.toggle('bad',!!bad);$('syncProgressFill').style.width=n+'%';$('syncProgressPct').textContent=n+'%';if(label)$('syncProgressLabel').textContent=label}
function syncBusy(on){$('seasonImportBtn').disabled=on;$('updateImportBtn').disabled=on;$('clearDatabaseBtn').disabled=on}
async function clearFMDatabase(){
 if(!confirm('Clear the imported FM database and reset this fantasy save, squad, points and leagues on this browser?'))return;
 syncBusy(true);
 try{await fmStoredClear();PLAYERS.splice(0);MATCHES.splice(0);SEASON_FIXTURES.splice(0);CHAMP_TABLE.splice(0);for(const k of Object.keys(STAR_TEAMS))delete STAR_TEAMS[k];for(const k of Object.keys(META))delete META[k];Object.assign(META,{named_players:0,hidden_unresolved:0,completed_gameweek:0,current_gameweek:1,total_matches_extracted:0,fully_completed_gameweeks:[]});state=fmDeepClone(DEFAULT);state.chips=fmDeepClone(DEFAULT.chips);localStorage.removeItem('fmfantasy_final_v5');localStorage.removeItem(FM_DEBUG_KEY);FM_DEBUG={version:'v25-pricing-guardrails',startedAt:null,mode:null,file:null,events:[],lastError:null,lastMeta:null};fixtureViewGW=1;starViewGW=1;teamViewGW=1;refreshCompetitionUI();refreshClubFilters();save();renderAll();$('syncProgress').classList.remove('show','bad');syncMessage('Database cleared — ready for a fresh Season / Database Import.','ok')}
 catch(e){syncMessage('Could not clear database: '+String(e?.message||e),'bad')}
 finally{syncBusy(false)}
}

async function sendFMImport(file,mode,autoRetry=false,explicitPreference=undefined){
 if(!file)return;
 if(mode==='season'&&state.teamConfirmed&&!confirm('Season / Database Import rebuilds the FM world and resets your fantasy squad. Continue?'))return;

 try{
  const old=await fmStoredGet();if(mode==='update'&&!old)throw new Error('Run Season / Database Import first.');

  if(result.duplicate){fmDebugAdd('result','Duplicate save detected — no database changes applied.');applyImportedPayload(old,'update');const processed=fmProcessCompletedGameweeks(),live=liveManagerGameweek();syncProgress(100,'Already up to date');syncMessage(processed.length?`Same save · repaired ${processed.length} completed Gameweek${processed.length===1?'':'s'} into your team total.`:live?`Same save · GW ${live.gw} remains ongoing at ${live.gross} live team pts.`:'Same save already imported — 0 database changes, nothing double-counted.','ok');return}
  let payload=result.payload,changes=null;

   changes=fmDiff(old,payload);const newIds=new Set((payload.players||[]).map(p=>String(p.pid))),retained=(old.players||[]).filter(p=>!newIds.has(String(p.pid))).map(p=>Object.assign({},p,{available:false,departed:true,visible:true}));if(retained.length){payload.players.push(...retained);payload.meta.historical_players_retained=retained.length}

  syncProgress(100,'Import complete');
  if(mode==='season'){
    const played=Number(payload.meta.played_results||0),rich=Number(payload.meta.rich_matches||0),missing=Math.max(0,played-rich);
    if(played>0&&missing>0)syncMessage(`${payload.meta.players} players · ${payload.meta.fixtures} fixtures · ${played} results loaded · player detail ${rich}/${played} matches (${missing} missing). Database loaded with partial historical player coverage — no automatic re-scan was run.`,'warn');
    else syncMessage(`${payload.meta.players} players · ${payload.meta.fixtures} fixtures · ${played} results loaded · player detail ${rich}/${played} matches. Historical coverage complete. Calendar Gameweeks ready.`,'ok');

 }catch(e){

function syncMessage(text,kind=''){const el=$('syncStatus');el.textContent=text;el.className='syncStatus '+kind}
function syncProgress(pct,label='',bad=false){const n=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(Number(pct)||0))),wrap=$('syncProgress');wrap.classList.add('show');wrap.classList.toggle('bad',!!bad);$('syncProgressFill').style.width=n+'%';$('syncProgressPct').textContent=n+'%';if(label)$('syncProgressLabel').textContent=label}
function syncBusy(on){$('seasonImportBtn').disabled=on;$('updateImportBtn').disabled=on;$('clearDatabaseBtn').disabled=on}
async function clearFMDatabase(){
 if(!confirm('Clear the imported FM database and reset this fantasy save, squad, points and leagues on this browser?'))return;
 syncBusy(true);
 try{await fmStoredClear();PLAYERS.splice(0);MATCHES.splice(0);SEASON_FIXTURES.splice(0);CHAMP_TABLE.splice(0);for(const k of Object.keys(STAR_TEAMS))delete STAR_TEAMS[k];for(const k of Object.keys(META))delete META[k];Object.assign(META,{named_players:0,hidden_unresolved:0,completed_gameweek:0,current_gameweek:1,total_matches_extracted:0,fully_completed_gameweeks:[]});state=fmDeepClone(DEFAULT);state.chips=fmDeepClone(DEFAULT.chips);localStorage.removeItem('fmfantasy_final_v5');localStorage.removeItem(FM_DEBUG_KEY);FM_DEBUG={version:'v25-pricing-guardrails',startedAt:null,mode:null,file:null,events:[],lastError:null,lastMeta:null};fixtureViewGW=1;starViewGW=1;teamViewGW=1;refreshCompetitionUI();refreshClubFilters();save();renderAll();$('syncProgress').classList.remove('show','bad');syncMessage('Database cleared — ready for a fresh Season / Database Import.','ok')}
 catch(e){syncMessage('Could not clear database: '+String(e?.message||e),'bad')}
 finally{syncBusy(false)}
}
let FM_PENDING_SEASON_LEAGUE=null;
async function sendFMImport(file,mode,autoRetry=false,explicitPreference=undefined){
 if(!file)return;
 if(mode==='season'&&state.teamConfirmed&&!confirm('Season / Database Import rebuilds the FM world and resets your fantasy squad. Continue?'))return;
 if(!autoRetry)fmDebugReset(file,mode);syncBusy(true);syncProgress(1,mode==='season'?'Starting season import…':'Starting Gameweek update…');syncMessage(mode==='season'?'Reading FM season/database…':'Checking FM gameweek update…');
 try{
  const old=await fmStoredGet();if(mode==='update'&&!old)throw new Error('Run Season / Database Import first.');
  const preferredLeague=mode==='update'?(old?.meta?.competition||null):(explicitPreference!==undefined?explicitPreference:(FM_PENDING_SEASON_LEAGUE!==null?FM_PENDING_SEASON_LEAGUE:($('leagueImportPreference')?.value||null)));fmDebugAdd('info',`League request: ${preferredLeague||'AUTO'}${mode==='season'?' (captured before file selection)':''}`);const result=await FM_RUNTIME.importFile(file,(t,pct)=>{syncMessage(t);syncProgress(pct,t);fmDebugAdd('progress',t,{percent:pct})},mode==='update'?old?.meta?.fingerprint:null,preferredLeague);
  if(result.duplicate){fmDebugAdd('result','Duplicate save detected — no database changes applied.');applyImportedPayload(old,'update');const processed=fmProcessCompletedGameweeks(),live=liveManagerGameweek();syncProgress(100,'Already up to date');syncMessage(processed.length?`Same save · repaired ${processed.length} completed Gameweek${processed.length===1?'':'s'} into your team total.`:live?`Same save · GW ${live.gw} remains ongoing at ${live.gross} live team pts.`:'Same save already imported — 0 database changes, nothing double-counted.','ok');return}
  let payload=result.payload,changes=null;
  if(mode==='update'){
   changes=fmDiff(old,payload);const newIds=new Set((payload.players||[]).map(p=>String(p.pid))),retained=(old.players||[]).filter(p=>!newIds.has(String(p.pid))).map(p=>Object.assign({},p,{available:false,departed:true,visible:true}));if(retained.length){payload.players.push(...retained);payload.meta.historical_players_retained=retained.length}
   fmMergeHistoricalFixtures(old,payload);fmMergeHistoricalMatches(old,payload);fmRebuildPlayerAggregates(payload);const priceMoves=fmApplyDynamicPricing(old,payload,changes);state.news=fmBuildImportNews(changes,priceMoves,payload,old);
  }else{fmInitialiseLaunchPrices(payload)}
  fmDeduplicateRichMatches(payload);fmRebuildPlayerAggregates(payload);payload.star_teams=fmBuildStars(payload.players||[],Number(payload.meta?.completed_gameweek||0));FM_DEBUG.lastMeta=payload.meta||null;fmDebugAdd('result','Payload built successfully.',payload.meta||{});try{const lm=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('fmfantasy_league_by_fingerprint_v1')||'{}');if(payload.meta?.fingerprint&&payload.meta?.competition){lm[payload.meta.fingerprint]=payload.meta.competition;localStorage.setItem('fmfantasy_league_by_fingerprint_v1',JSON.stringify(lm))}}catch(_e){};syncProgress(99,'Applying imported database…');await fmStoredSet(payload);applyImportedPayload(payload,mode);const processed=mode==='update'?fmProcessCompletedGameweeks():[];const live=mode==='update'?liveManagerGameweek():null;
  syncProgress(100,'Import complete');
  if(mode==='season'){
    const played=Number(payload.meta.played_results||0),rich=Number(payload.meta.rich_matches||0),missing=Math.max(0,played-rich);
    if(played>0&&missing>0)syncMessage(`${payload.meta.players} players · ${payload.meta.fixtures} fixtures · ${played} results loaded · player detail ${rich}/${played} matches (${missing} missing). Database loaded with partial historical player coverage — no automatic re-scan was run.`,'warn');
    else syncMessage(`${payload.meta.players} players · ${payload.meta.fixtures} fixtures · ${played} results loaded · player detail ${rich}/${played} matches. Historical coverage complete. Calendar Gameweeks ready.`,'ok');
  }
  else{const pts=processed.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.net||0),0);syncMessage(`${changes.new_results.length} new results · ${changes.new_rich_matches} new rich matches · ${processed.length} Gameweek${processed.length===1?'':'s'} finalised${processed.length?` · ${pts} net pts`:''}${live?` · GW ${live.gw} ongoing: ${live.gross} live team pts`:''}.`,'ok')}
 }catch(e){
  const msg=String(e?.message||e||'');console.error(e);FM_DEBUG.lastError=fmDebugSafeError(e);fmDebugAdd('error',FM_DEBUG.lastError.message);fmDebugPersist();
  if(mode==='season'&&!autoRetry&&!($('leagueImportPreference')?.value)&&msg.includes('Multiple supported English league seasons')){
    const isPrem=confirm('Auto-detect found both Premier League and Championship data in this FM save and cannot safely guess the human-managed league.\n\nPress OK for Premier League.\nPress Cancel for EFL Championship.\n\nThe same save will retry automatically — you do not need to upload it again.');
    const chosen=isPrem?'Premier League':'EFL Championship';$('leagueImportPreference').value=chosen;fmDebugAdd('info',`Auto-detect ambiguity resolved in-page as ${chosen}; retrying the same file.`);syncProgress(1,`Retrying as ${chosen}…`);syncMessage(`Auto-detect needed one safe league choice. Retrying as ${chosen}…`);
    return await sendFMImport(file,mode,true,chosen);
  }
  syncProgress(Number($('syncProgressPct').textContent.replace('%',''))||0,'Import failed',true);syncMessage(fmFriendlyError(e)+' · Use Export Debug to copy the full traceback.','bad')
 }
 finally{syncBusy(false);$('seasonImportFile').value='';$('updateImportFile').value=''}
}

}

### oldPrice hit 1
ent=`GW ${META.completed_gameweek||0}`;$('sideTeam').textContent=state.teamConfirmed?state.teamName:'Not entered';$('sideFT').textContent=!state.teamConfirmed||!rulesActive()?'Unlimited':`${state.freeTransfers}/5 FT`;$('sidePts').textContent=state.teamConfirmed?managerDisplayTotal():(state.totalPoints||0)}

function fitActivePage(){
 const main=document.querySelector('.main'),top=document.querySelector('.top'),page=document.querySelector('.page.active');
 if(!main||!top||!page)return;
 page.style.zoom='1';page.style.width='100%';
 const mt=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(page).marginTop||0)||0;
 const availH=Math.max(120,main.clientHeight-top.offsetHeight-mt-4);
 const availW=Math.max(240,main.clientWidth);
 let naturalH=Math.max(1,page.scrollHeight),naturalW=Math.max(1,page.scrollWidth);
 let scale=Math.min(1,availH/naturalH,availW/naturalW);
 if(scale<.999){
   page.style.zoom=String(scale);
   page.style.width=(100/scale)+'%';
   // Re-measure once after widening because responsive grids may become shorter.
   requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
     page.style.zoom='1';
     const h2=Math.max(1,page.scrollHeight),w2=Math.max(1,page.scrollWidth);
     let s2=Math.min(1,availH/h2,availW/w2);
     page.style.zoom=String(s2);
     page.style.width=(100/s2)+'%';
   });
 }
}

function refreshCompetitionUI(){const max=seasonGWMax(),label=leagueLabel();if($('competitionTopLabel'))$('competitionTopLabel').textContent=`FM Fantasy ${label}`;if($('leagueTableHeading'))$('leagueTableHeading').textContent=label;if($('fixtureCalendarLabel'))$('fixtureCalendarLabel').textContent=`${max} calendar Gameweeks`;if($('leagueImportPreference'))$('leagueImportPreference').dataset.loadedCompetition=META.competition||'';const sel=$('fixtureGW');if(sel){const keep=Math.max(1,Math.min(max,Number(sel.value||state.currentGameweek||1)));sel.innerHTML=Array.from({length:max},(_,i)=>`<option value="${i+1}">GW ${i+1}</option>`).join('');sel.value=String(keep)}}
function refreshClubFilters(){
 for(const id of ['clubFilter','fixtureClub']){const el=$(id);if(!el)continue;const keep=el.value;el.innerHTML='<option value="">All clubs</option>';for(const c of [...new Set(PLAYERS.filter(p=>p.visible&&p.available!==false).map(p=>p.club))].sort())el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<option>${esc(c)}</option>`);if([...el.options].some(o=>o.value===keep))el.value=keep}
}

function fmHistoryKey(h){return `${h?.date||''}|${h?.home||''}|${h?.away||''}|${h?.opponent||''}|${h?.venue||''}`}
function fmPriceTrackerSeed(p,gw){return{samples:[],consecutiveStarts:0,consecutiveMisses:0,absenceCheckpoint:0,startCheckpoint:0,lastHistoryCount:(p.history||[]).length,baselineNailedness:Number(p.price_context?.nailedness||0),baselineRole:String(p.price_context?.availability_signal||''),baselineGameweek:Number(gw||0),processedHistoryKeys:[...new Set((p.history||[]).map(fmHistoryKey))],importsObserved:0}}
function fmInitialiseLaunchPrices(payload){const gw=Number(payload?.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0);for(const p of payload.players||[]){p.model_price=Number(p.price||0);p.launch_price=Number(p.price||0);p.dynamic_price=Number(p.price||0);p.price=Number(p.price||0);p.price_baseline_gw=gw;p.price_change_history=[];p.price_tracker=fmPriceTrackerSeed(p,gw);}payload.meta=payload.meta||{};payload.meta.price_baseline_gw=gw;payload.meta.price_policy='launch price frozen at database import; only post-import sustained form can move price';}
function fmPriceSample(h){const mins=Number(h?.minutes||0),pts=Number(h?.fpl_points||0),ga=Number(h?.goals||0)+Number(h?.assists||0),rating=Number(h?.rating||0);if(mins<=0)return null;if(ga>0||pts>=7||rating>=7.4)return 1;if(mins>=60&&ga===0&&pts<=2&&(rating===0||rating<=6.6))return -1;return 0}
function fmApplyDynamicPricing(oldP,payload,changes){const oldMap=new Map((oldP.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),inc=[],dec=[];for(const p of payload.players||[]){const o=oldMap.get(String(p.pid));p.model_price=Number(p.price||0);if(!o){p.launch_price=Number(p.price||0);p.dynamic_price=Number(p.price||0);p.price_baseline_gw=Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0);p.price_change_history=[];p.price_tracker=fmPriceTrackerSeed(p,p.price_baseline_gw);continue}
 const oldPrice=Number((o.price??o.dynamic_price??o.launch_price??p.price)||0);let tracker=fmDeepClone(o.price_tracker||fmPriceTrackerSeed(o,o.price_baseline_gw??oldP.meta?.price_baseline_gw??0));if(!Array.isArray(tracker.processedHistoryKeys)){tracker.processedHistoryKeys=[...new Set((o.history||[]).map(fmHistoryKey))];tracker.samples=[];tracker.baselineGameweek=Number(o.price_baseline_gw??oldP.meta?.price_baseline_gw??0)}const processed=new Set(tracker.processedHistoryKeys||[]);const newRows=(p.history||[]).filter(h=>!processed.has(fmHistoryKey(h))).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date||'').localeCompare(String(b.date||'')));const clubNew=(changes?.new_results||[]).filter(f=>f.home===p.club||f.away===p.club).length,playedNew=newRows.filter(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0),startedNew=newRows.filter(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>=60);
 for(const h of newRows){const sm=fmPriceSample(h);if(sm!==null)tracker.samples.push(sm);processed.add(fmHistoryKey(h))}tracker.processedHistoryKeys=[...processed].slice(-80);tracker.samples=tracker.samples.slice(-10);tracker.importsObserved=Number(tracker.importsObserved||0)+1;
 if(clubNew){if(playedNew.length){tracker.consecutiveMisses=0;tracker.consecutiveStarts=startedNew.length===clubNew?Number(tracker.consecutiveStarts||0)+startedNew.length:startedNew.length;if(startedNew.length<clubNew)tracker.startCheckpoint=0}else{tracker.consecutiveStarts=0;tracker.startCheckpoint=0;tracker.consecutiveMisses=Number(tracker.consecutiveMisses||0)+clubNew}}
 let delta=0;const samples=tracker.samples,baseN=Number(tracker.baselineNailedness||0),baselineBackup=baseN<0.38||/backup/i.test(String(tracker.baselineRole||''));
 // The database import is the hard baseline. Historic rows already present at launch are never form samples.
 // A single post-import match can never move price: sustained evidence is required.
 if(samples.length>=3){const last3=samples.slice(-3),sum3=last3.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);if(sum3>=3)delta+=0.2;else if(sum3>=2)delta+=0.1}
 if(baselineBackup){const starts=Number(tracker.consecutiveStarts||0),done=Number(tracker.startCheckpoint||0);let target=done;if(starts>=10)target=Math.max(target,10);else if(starts>=5)target=Math.max(target,5);if(target>done){delta=Math.max(delta,target>=10?0.2:0.2);tracker.startCheckpoint=target}}
 if(samples.length>=5){const last5=samples.slice(-5),sum5=last5.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);if(sum5<=-4)delta=Math.min(delta,-0.2);else if(sum5<=-3)delta=Math.min(delta,-0.1)}
 if(!baselineBackup&&tracker.consecutiveMisses>=4){const targetSteps=Math.floor((tracker.consecutiveMisses-2)/2),done=Number(tracker.absenceCheckpoint||0);if(targetSteps>done){delta-=0.1*(targetSteps-done);tracker.absenceCheckpoint=targetSteps}}
 if(playedNew.length)tracker.absenceCheckpoint=0;
 // Never alter prices on the very first Gameweek Update after the launch database.
 // This guarantees pre-launch history cannot leak into market movement.
 if(Number(tracker.importsObserved||0)<2)delta=0;
 // Weekly market movement stays gradual even if a save import contains more than one newly-played fixture.
 const maxMove=Math.max(0.1,Math.min(0.2,0.1*Math.max(1,Math.min(2,clubNew||newRows.length||1))));delta=Math.max(-maxMove,Math.min(maxMove,delta));delta=Math.round(delta*10)/10;
 if(Math.abs(delta)>=0.099)tracker.samples=[];const newPrice=Math.round(Math.max(3.5,Math.min(15,oldPrice+delta))*10)/10;p.launch_price=Number(o.launch_price??oldPrice);p.dynamic_price=newPrice;p.price=newPrice;p.price_baseline_gw=Number(o.price_baseline_gw??oldP.meta?.price_baseline_gw??0);p.price_tracker=tracker;p.price_change_history=[...(o.price_change_history||[])];if(Math.abs(newPrice-oldPrice)>=0.099){const item={pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,old_price:oldPrice,new_price:newPrice,delta:Math.round((newPrice-oldPrice)*10)/10};p.price_change_history.push({...item,at:new Date().toISOString(),gw:Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0)});p.price_change_history=p.price_change_history.slice(-30);(newPrice>oldPrice?inc:dec).push(item)} }
 payload.meta.price_policy='launch price frozen at database import; only post-import sustained form can move price';return{increases:inc.sort((a,b)=>b.delta-a.delta),decreases:dec.sort((a,b)=>a.delta-b.delta)} }
function fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload){const ids=new Set();const byClub=new Map();for(const p of payload?.players||[]){if(p.available===false||p.visible===false)continue;const arr=byClub.get(p.club)||[];arr.push(p);byClub.set(p.club,arr);if((p.history||[]).length||Number(p.minutes||0)>0||p.first_team===true||/senior|first/i.test(String(p.squad_type||p.squad_role||'')))ids.add(String(p.pid))}for(const arr of byClub.values()){arr.sort((a,b)=>Number(b.ca||0)-Number(a.ca||0));for(const p of arr.slice(0,18))ids.add(String(p.pid))}return ids}
function fmTransferValue(p){const v=p?.market_value??p?.transfer_value??p?.fm_value??p?.value;if(v===undefined||v===null||v==='')return '—';if(typeof v==='number'){if(v>=1000000)return `£${(v/1000000).toFixed(v>=10000000?1:2)}m`;if(v>=1000)return `£${Math.round(v/1000)}k`;return `£${v.toLocaleString('en-GB')}`}return String(v)}
function fmFmtStatusDate(v){if(!v)return'';try{const d=new Date(String(v).length<=10?String(v)+'T12:00:00':v);if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return String(v);return d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:d.getFullYear()===new Date().getFullYear()?undefined:'numeric'})}catch(_e){return String(v)}}
function fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,club,date){return (payload.fixtures||[]).filter(f=>f.status==='played'&&String(f.date||'')>String(date||'')&&(f.home===club||f.away===club)).length}
function fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const explicit=Number(p.suspension_remaining??p.suspension_games_remaining??p.ban_games_remaining??0),status=String(p.suspension_status??'').toLowerCase(),until=p.banned_until??p.suspension_until??null;if(explicit>0||/(suspend|ban|unavailable)/.test(status)||until){const bits=['Suspended'];if(explicit>0)bits.push(`${explicit} league match${explicit===1?'':'es'} remaining`);else if(until)bits.push(`until ${fmFmtStatusDate(until)}`);const reason=String(p.suspension_detail??'').trim();if(reason&&!/^active fm suspension$/i.test(reason))bits.push(reason);out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:bits.join(' · ')});continue}const hist=[...(p.history||[])].filter(h=>h.date).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));if(!hist.length)continue;const incidents=[];for(const h of hist){const rc=Number(h.red_cards??h.rc??0),yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);if(rc>0)incidents.push({date:String(h.date),ban:1,reason:yc>=2?'second-yellow red':'red card'});}let yellows=[];for(const h of hist){const yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);for(let i=0;i<yc;i++)yellows.push(String(h.date));}for(const [threshold,ban] of [[5,1],[10,2],[15,3]])if(yellows.length>=threshold)incidents.push({date:yellows[threshold-1],ban,reason:`${threshold} yellow cards`});incidents.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));for(const inc of incidents){const served=fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,p.club,inc.date),remaining=Math.max(0,Number(inc.ban)-served);if(remaining>0){out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:`Suspended · ${remaining} league match${remaining===1?'':'es'} remaining · ${inc.reason}`});break}}}return out}
function fmInferActiveInjuries(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const raw=p.injury_status??p.injury??p.injury_name??(p.injured?'Injured':null);if(!raw)continue;const text=String(raw);if(/^(fit|none|false|0)$/i.test(text)||/recovered|fully fit|available/i.test(text))continue;const back=p.injury_return_date??p.expected_return_date??p.injury_expected_back??p.injury_end_date??null;const detail=`${/^injur/i.test(text)?text:`Injured · ${text}`}${back?` · expected back ${fmFmtStatusDate(back)}`:''}`;out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail})}return out}
function fmBuildInitialNews(payload){const active={injuries:fmInferActiveInjuries(payload),suspensions:fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload)};state.activeStatuses=active;return{generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),gameweek:Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0),initial:true,transfers:[],priceIncreases:[],priceDecreases:[],injuries:active.injuries,suspensions:active.suspensions}}
function fmBuildImportNews(changes,priceMoves,payload,oldP){const oldMap=new Map((oldP?.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),newMap=new Map((payload.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),seniorNew=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload),seniorOld=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(oldP||{});const transfers=[],seenTransfers=new Set();const seniorEvidence=p=>{if(!p)return false;const ctx=p.price_context||{},played=Number(p.minutes||0)>0||(p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0),rank=Number(ctx.usage_rank||99),quality=Number(ctx.quality||0),nailed=Number(ctx.nailedness||0);return played||p.first_team===true||/senior|
### oldPrice hit 2
price=Number(p.price||0);p.launch_price=Number(p.price||0);p.dynamic_price=Number(p.price||0);p.price=Number(p.price||0);p.price_baseline_gw=gw;p.price_change_history=[];p.price_tracker=fmPriceTrackerSeed(p,gw);}payload.meta=payload.meta||{};payload.meta.price_baseline_gw=gw;payload.meta.price_policy='launch price frozen at database import; only post-import sustained form can move price';}
function fmPriceSample(h){const mins=Number(h?.minutes||0),pts=Number(h?.fpl_points||0),ga=Number(h?.goals||0)+Number(h?.assists||0),rating=Number(h?.rating||0);if(mins<=0)return null;if(ga>0||pts>=7||rating>=7.4)return 1;if(mins>=60&&ga===0&&pts<=2&&(rating===0||rating<=6.6))return -1;return 0}
function fmApplyDynamicPricing(oldP,payload,changes){const oldMap=new Map((oldP.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),inc=[],dec=[];for(const p of payload.players||[]){const o=oldMap.get(String(p.pid));p.model_price=Number(p.price||0);if(!o){p.launch_price=Number(p.price||0);p.dynamic_price=Number(p.price||0);p.price_baseline_gw=Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0);p.price_change_history=[];p.price_tracker=fmPriceTrackerSeed(p,p.price_baseline_gw);continue}
 const oldPrice=Number((o.price??o.dynamic_price??o.launch_price??p.price)||0);let tracker=fmDeepClone(o.price_tracker||fmPriceTrackerSeed(o,o.price_baseline_gw??oldP.meta?.price_baseline_gw??0));if(!Array.isArray(tracker.processedHistoryKeys)){tracker.processedHistoryKeys=[...new Set((o.history||[]).map(fmHistoryKey))];tracker.samples=[];tracker.baselineGameweek=Number(o.price_baseline_gw??oldP.meta?.price_baseline_gw??0)}const processed=new Set(tracker.processedHistoryKeys||[]);const newRows=(p.history||[]).filter(h=>!processed.has(fmHistoryKey(h))).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date||'').localeCompare(String(b.date||'')));const clubNew=(changes?.new_results||[]).filter(f=>f.home===p.club||f.away===p.club).length,playedNew=newRows.filter(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0),startedNew=newRows.filter(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>=60);
 for(const h of newRows){const sm=fmPriceSample(h);if(sm!==null)tracker.samples.push(sm);processed.add(fmHistoryKey(h))}tracker.processedHistoryKeys=[...processed].slice(-80);tracker.samples=tracker.samples.slice(-10);tracker.importsObserved=Number(tracker.importsObserved||0)+1;
 if(clubNew){if(playedNew.length){tracker.consecutiveMisses=0;tracker.consecutiveStarts=startedNew.length===clubNew?Number(tracker.consecutiveStarts||0)+startedNew.length:startedNew.length;if(startedNew.length<clubNew)tracker.startCheckpoint=0}else{tracker.consecutiveStarts=0;tracker.startCheckpoint=0;tracker.consecutiveMisses=Number(tracker.consecutiveMisses||0)+clubNew}}
 let delta=0;const samples=tracker.samples,baseN=Number(tracker.baselineNailedness||0),baselineBackup=baseN<0.38||/backup/i.test(String(tracker.baselineRole||''));
 // The database import is the hard baseline. Historic rows already present at launch are never form samples.
 // A single post-import match can never move price: sustained evidence is required.
 if(samples.length>=3){const last3=samples.slice(-3),sum3=last3.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);if(sum3>=3)delta+=0.2;else if(sum3>=2)delta+=0.1}
 if(baselineBackup){const starts=Number(tracker.consecutiveStarts||0),done=Number(tracker.startCheckpoint||0);let target=done;if(starts>=10)target=Math.max(target,10);else if(starts>=5)target=Math.max(target,5);if(target>done){delta=Math.max(delta,target>=10?0.2:0.2);tracker.startCheckpoint=target}}
 if(samples.length>=5){const last5=samples.slice(-5),sum5=last5.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);if(sum5<=-4)delta=Math.min(delta,-0.2);else if(sum5<=-3)delta=Math.min(delta,-0.1)}
 if(!baselineBackup&&tracker.consecutiveMisses>=4){const targetSteps=Math.floor((tracker.consecutiveMisses-2)/2),done=Number(tracker.absenceCheckpoint||0);if(targetSteps>done){delta-=0.1*(targetSteps-done);tracker.absenceCheckpoint=targetSteps}}
 if(playedNew.length)tracker.absenceCheckpoint=0;
 // Never alter prices on the very first Gameweek Update after the launch database.
 // This guarantees pre-launch history cannot leak into market movement.
 if(Number(tracker.importsObserved||0)<2)delta=0;
 // Weekly market movement stays gradual even if a save import contains more than one newly-played fixture.
 const maxMove=Math.max(0.1,Math.min(0.2,0.1*Math.max(1,Math.min(2,clubNew||newRows.length||1))));delta=Math.max(-maxMove,Math.min(maxMove,delta));delta=Math.round(delta*10)/10;
 if(Math.abs(delta)>=0.099)tracker.samples=[];const newPrice=Math.round(Math.max(3.5,Math.min(15,oldPrice+delta))*10)/10;p.launch_price=Number(o.launch_price??oldPrice);p.dynamic_price=newPrice;p.price=newPrice;p.price_baseline_gw=Number(o.price_baseline_gw??oldP.meta?.price_baseline_gw??0);p.price_tracker=tracker;p.price_change_history=[...(o.price_change_history||[])];if(Math.abs(newPrice-oldPrice)>=0.099){const item={pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,old_price:oldPrice,new_price:newPrice,delta:Math.round((newPrice-oldPrice)*10)/10};p.price_change_history.push({...item,at:new Date().toISOString(),gw:Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0)});p.price_change_history=p.price_change_history.slice(-30);(newPrice>oldPrice?inc:dec).push(item)} }
 payload.meta.price_policy='launch price frozen at database import; only post-import sustained form can move price';return{increases:inc.sort((a,b)=>b.delta-a.delta),decreases:dec.sort((a,b)=>a.delta-b.delta)} }
function fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload){const ids=new Set();const byClub=new Map();for(const p of payload?.players||[]){if(p.available===false||p.visible===false)continue;const arr=byClub.get(p.club)||[];arr.push(p);byClub.set(p.club,arr);if((p.history||[]).length||Number(p.minutes||0)>0||p.first_team===true||/senior|first/i.test(String(p.squad_type||p.squad_role||'')))ids.add(String(p.pid))}for(const arr of byClub.values()){arr.sort((a,b)=>Number(b.ca||0)-Number(a.ca||0));for(const p of arr.slice(0,18))ids.add(String(p.pid))}return ids}
function fmTransferValue(p){const v=p?.market_value??p?.transfer_value??p?.fm_value??p?.value;if(v===undefined||v===null||v==='')return '—';if(typeof v==='number'){if(v>=1000000)return `£${(v/1000000).toFixed(v>=10000000?1:2)}m`;if(v>=1000)return `£${Math.round(v/1000)}k`;return `£${v.toLocaleString('en-GB')}`}return String(v)}
function fmFmtStatusDate(v){if(!v)return'';try{const d=new Date(String(v).length<=10?String(v)+'T12:00:00':v);if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return String(v);return d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB',{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:d.getFullYear()===new Date().getFullYear()?undefined:'numeric'})}catch(_e){return String(v)}}
function fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,club,date){return (payload.fixtures||[]).filter(f=>f.status==='played'&&String(f.date||'')>String(date||'')&&(f.home===club||f.away===club)).length}
function fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const explicit=Number(p.suspension_remaining??p.suspension_games_remaining??p.ban_games_remaining??0),status=String(p.suspension_status??'').toLowerCase(),until=p.banned_until??p.suspension_until??null;if(explicit>0||/(suspend|ban|unavailable)/.test(status)||until){const bits=['Suspended'];if(explicit>0)bits.push(`${explicit} league match${explicit===1?'':'es'} remaining`);else if(until)bits.push(`until ${fmFmtStatusDate(until)}`);const reason=String(p.suspension_detail??'').trim();if(reason&&!/^active fm suspension$/i.test(reason))bits.push(reason);out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:bits.join(' · ')});continue}const hist=[...(p.history||[])].filter(h=>h.date).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));if(!hist.length)continue;const incidents=[];for(const h of hist){const rc=Number(h.red_cards??h.rc??0),yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);if(rc>0)incidents.push({date:String(h.date),ban:1,reason:yc>=2?'second-yellow red':'red card'});}let yellows=[];for(const h of hist){const yc=Number(h.yellow_cards??h.yc??0);for(let i=0;i<yc;i++)yellows.push(String(h.date));}for(const [threshold,ban] of [[5,1],[10,2],[15,3]])if(yellows.length>=threshold)incidents.push({date:yellows[threshold-1],ban,reason:`${threshold} yellow cards`});incidents.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));for(const inc of incidents){const served=fmClubPlayedAfter(payload,p.club,inc.date),remaining=Math.max(0,Number(inc.ban)-served);if(remaining>0){out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail:`Suspended · ${remaining} league match${remaining===1?'':'es'} remaining · ${inc.reason}`});break}}}return out}
function fmInferActiveInjuries(payload){const out=[];for(const p of payload?.players||[]){const raw=p.injury_status??p.injury??p.injury_name??(p.injured?'Injured':null);if(!raw)continue;const text=String(raw);if(/^(fit|none|false|0)$/i.test(text)||/recovered|fully fit|available/i.test(text))continue;const back=p.injury_return_date??p.expected_return_date??p.injury_expected_back??p.injury_end_date??null;const detail=`${/^injur/i.test(text)?text:`Injured · ${text}`}${back?` · expected back ${fmFmtStatusDate(back)}`:''}`;out.push({pid:String(p.pid),name:playerName(p),club:p.club,pos:p.pos,detail})}return out}

function fmBuildImportNews(changes,priceMoves,payload,oldP){const oldMap=new Map((oldP?.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),newMap=new Map((payload.players||[]).map(p=>[String(p.pid),p])),seniorNew=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(payload),seniorOld=fmFirstTeamNewsIds(oldP||{});const transfers=[],seenTransfers=new Set();const seniorEvidence=p=>{if(!p)return false;const ctx=p.price_context||{},played=Number(p.minutes||0)>0||(p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0),rank=Number(ctx.usage_rank||99),quality=Number(ctx.quality||0),nailed=Number(ctx.nailedness||0);return played||p.first_team===true||/senior|first/i.test(String(p.squad_type||p.squad_role||''))||(rank<=18&&quality>=.48&&nailed>=.35)};const addTransfer=(x,p,from,to,kind='move')=>{if(!p)return;const id=String(p.pid);if(!seniorNew.has(id)&&!seniorOld.has(id))return;if(!seniorEvidence(p))return;if(kind==='added'&&!((p.history||[]).some(h=>Number(h.minutes||0)>0)||Number(p.minutes||0)>0||((p.price_context?.usage_rank||99)<=16&&Number(p.price_context?.quality||0)>=.62&&Number(p.price_context?.nailedness||0)>=.48)))return;const k=`${id}|${from}|${to}`;if(seenTransfers.has(k))return;seenTransfers.add(k);transfers.push({name:x?.name||playerName(p),old_club:from||'Outside league',new_club:to||p.club||'',club:to||p.club||'',pos:p.pos||'',value:fmTransferValue(p),price:Number(p.price||0)})};for(const x of changes?.players_moved||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,x.from,x.to||p?.club,'move')}for(const x of changes?.players_added||[]){const p=newMap.get(String(x.pid));addTransfer(x,p,'Outside league',p?.club,'added')}const active={injuries:fmInferActiveInjuries(payload),suspensions:fmInferActiveSuspensions(payload)};state.activeStatuses=active;return{generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),gameweek:Number(payload.meta?.latest_gameweek_with_result||0),transfers,priceIncreases:priceMoves?.increases||[],priceDecreases:priceMoves?.decreases||[],injuries:active.injuries,suspensions:active.suspensions}}
function newsRow(item,type){if(type==='price')return `<div class="newsRow" ${item.pid?`data-open-player="${esc(item.pid)}"`:'' } data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.club} ${item.pos}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.club)}</span><span>${esc(item.pos)}</span><span><b>£${Number(item.old_price).toFixed(1)}</b> → <b class="${item.new_price>item.old_price?'newsPriceUp':'newsPriceDown'}">£${Number(item.new_price).toFixed(1)}</b></span></div>`;if(type==='transfer')return `<div class="newsRow transfer" data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.old_club||''} ${item.new_club||item.club||''} ${item.pos||''}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.old_club||item.from||'Outside league')}</span><span>${esc(item.new_club||item.to||item.club||'')}</span><span>${esc(item.value||'—')}</span><span>${esc(item.pos||'')}</span><span>£${Number(item.price||0).toFixed(1)}m</span></div>`;return `<div class="newsRow status" ${item.pid?`data-open-player="${esc(item.pid)}"`:'' } data-news-text="${esc(`${item.name} ${item.club} ${item.pos} ${item.detail||''}`.toLowerCase())}"><b>${esc(item.name)}</b><span>${esc(item.club)}</span><span>${esc(item.pos)}</span><span>${esc(item.detail||'Update')}</span></div>`}
